Compute gradient for given weights. We can loop over d with w. Let d from -(n-1) to n-1. For each with weight wsignificant:
 if d==0: grad += w*(1-2h)
 else:
   if d>0:
      # term (1-h_{j+d}) for j=0..n-d-1
      grad[:n-d] += w*(1-h[d:])
      # term -h_{j-d} for j=d..n-1
      grad[d:] += -w*h[:-d]
   else:
      e=-d
      # c_{-e}=∑_{j=e}^{n-1} h_j(1-h_{j-e})
      # derivative (1-h_{j-e}) for j=e..n-1
      grad[e:] += w*(1-h[:-e])
      # derivative -h_{j+e} for j=0..n-e-1
      grad[:n-e] += -w*h[e:]
This gives ∂c_full? Need verify with c from np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full'). For d = m-(n-1). If m=0, d=-(n-1): c[0] = h_{n-1}(1-h_0)? Let's design exact pair feasibility:
- Current h, base c. Pair i,j, a0,b0, s. δ variable. Affected m list unique.
- For each m, compute base, L, Q.
- Unaffected max U = max c[~mask]. If U > T => infeasible.
- For each affected constraint:
   Q=0: interval: if base + Lδ ≤ T. If L>0: δ ≤ (T-base)/L; if L<0: δ ≥ (T-base)/L; if L=0: require base≤T.
   Q=+1: base + Lδ + δ^2 ≤T => δ^2+Lδ+(base-T)≤0. Quadratic diff = L^2 -4(base-T). If diff<0: empty. Roots r1=(-L - sqrt(diff))/2, r2=(-L + sqrt(diff))/2; interval [r1,r2].
   Q=-1: base + Lδ - δ^2 ≤T => -δ^2+Lδ+(base-T)≤0 => δ^2 -Lδ + (T-base) ≥0. Let f=δ^2 -Lδ +(T-base). diff = L^2 -4(T-base). If diff<0: f>0 always? If diff<0, quadratic positive (since leading +) => all δ feasible. If diff>=0, roots r1,r2 (r1≤r2), feasible δ≤r1 or δ≥r2.
- Feasible δ initially intersection of domain [δlo,δhi] with interval list. Implement maintain list of intervals (non-overlapping sorted). Start [(δlo,δhi)]. Apply each constraint by intersecting with feasible set of that constraint. For Q=-1, feasible complement of (r1,r2) within domain. Intersect intervals: For each existing interval [a,b], intersect with (-∞,r1] and [r2,∞). Can split. Number intervals may grow? Complement of interval applied to multiple intervals can split each; start 1, after one outside constraint up to 2. Subsequent interval constraints (connected) intersect each interval, won't increase beyond 2 if intervals remain separated and each constraint dominated by connected interval or complement? Intersecting two disjoint intervals with a connected interval can leave up to 2. Complement can turn each into up to 2 -> up to 4 if applied again, but only one complement constraint (m=n-1) maybe if affected; so at most 2. Good.
- Feasibility for T if final intervals nonempty.
- Binary search T between U and current max C_base? current δ=0 feasible with T=current_max. Lower maybe min(max(U, min_affected?)) or 0. Iterate 40-60 to precision.
- After find T, recover feasible δ at T: compute intervals for T (maybe T=low optimal). Choose δ in first interval clipped to domain. To improve, choose δ that minimizes current max? Implementation of feasible(T):
```
def pair_check(T, base, L, Q, lo, hi):
    l=lo; h=hi; eps=1e-14
    # Q=0
    m0 = Q==0
    if np.any(m0):
       L0=L[m0]; b0=base[m0]
       zero = L0==0
       if np.any(zero) and np.any(b0[zero] > T + 1e-15): return False,None
       pos = L0>0
       if np.any(pos): h = min(h, float(np.min((T-b0[pos])/L0[pos])))
       neg = L0<0
       if np.any(neg): l = max(l, float(np.max((T-b0[neg])/L0[neg])))
       if h < l - 1e-14: return False,None
    # Q>0
    m1 = Q>0
    if np.any(m1):
       L1=L[m1]; b1=base[m1]; Q1=Q[m1]
       diff = L1*L1 - 4*Q1*(b1-T)
       if np.any(diff < -1e-14): return False,None
       sd = np.sqrt(np.maximum(diff,0.0))
       r1 = (-L1 - sd)/(2*Q1)
       r2 = (-L1 + sd)/(2*Q1)
       l = max(l, float(np.max(r1)))
       h = min(h, float(np.min(r2)))
       if h < l - eps: return False,None
    # Q<0 (only -2 but generic)
    m2 = Q<0
    if np.any(m2):
       L2=L[m2]; b2=base[m2]; A2=-Q2[m2]
       D = L2*L2 - 4*A2*(T-b2)
       # Need all constraints: feasible is intersection of complements of (r1,r2) for D>0. Multiple concave constraints! There can be only one (diag), but generic if multiple would be intersection of multiple outside intervals -> potentially many intervals. But only one diagonal. We can handle one.
```
Could there be multiple Q<0? Only m=n-1. So fine. If multiple, need interval split. But we know one. We can find idx = np.where(Q<0)[0]; if len>1 maybe handle conservatively? Could treat as two? Not necessary.
For m2 single:
       D... if D <= 1e-14: all feasible.
       else roots r1=(-L - sqrtD)/(2A), r2=(-L + sqrtD)/(2A)
       # Need [l,h] not entirely inside (r1,r2). Feasible if l <= r1 or r2 <= h. Use eps.
       if not (r1 >= l - eps or r2 <= h + eps): return False
       # To recover, we can store r1,r2.
```

def pair_solve(base,L,Q,lo,hi):
   # upper current max feasible T_hi = max(base + L*0+Q*0 for all? current max c max) = hiT = max(base)
   # lower hi? Use max(min possible?) maybe max(U, min_)
   loT = 0.0
   hiT = float(np.max(base))  # current C/dx
   # We only want lower than current; can set hiT=hiT
   for _ in range(45):
      T=(loT+hiT)/2
      ok, info = check(T)
      if ok: hiT=T; last_info=info
      else: loT=T
   T=hiT
   recompute info at hiT
   choose delta
```

def pair_search(h, c, n, deadline, max_iters=??):
   best_c = c.max()
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time>deadline break
      if no_accept in last 2000: break?
      sample candidate pairs:
        - use top shifts: m_top = np.argsort(c)[-K:] (excluding diag? include)
        For each m_top, d=m_top-(n-1)
        if d>0: random i in [0,n-d-1], pair (i,i+d)
        if d<0: e=-d, random j in [e,n-1], pair (j-e,j)
        if d==0: random i,j (for c0 pair sum preserve)
        Also random pairs.
     for each candidate (unique maybe):
        call pair_solve; if delta !=0 and improved, accept, recompute top? 
        if new_max < best_c: best_c = new_max; no_accept=0; update_random etc
      maybe after each accepted, recompute c with correlate? We update c exactly, but to avoid drift, after e.g. 100 accepts or if c max inconsistent, recompute c = correlate.
   return h,c,max
```

Generic 1D minimax with quadratics: Feasibility for T: start intervals [lo,hi]. For each constraint m:
- Q=0: interval (connected) intersect.
- Q>0: interval intersect.
- Q<0: feasible complement of open interval (r1,r2), can split each existing interval into up to two => intervals count up to double. But after many, can blow. However we can perform all Q>0/0 first to get a single interval [l,h] (intersection connected). Then apply Q<0 constraints one by one. The feasible set after applying multiple complements is intersection of [l,h] with union of k intervals. This can have at most k+1 intervals. k number of negative constraints. We can track list and if becomes large, maybe use dense grid fallback. For s=20, Q negative count could be many (same sign pairs distances). But maybe not too large. We can also treat Q<0 constraints by adding their excluded intervals and then check if [l,h] minus union of excluded intervals nonempty. That's easier and exact! Since feasible for concave constraints is complement of (r1,r2). We need [l,h] not entirely covered by union of excluded intervals. We can collect all excluded intervals (open) for constraints with Q<0 and D>0. Then compute if there exists point in [l,h] not in union. This is O(k log k). This handles multiple concave. Great. For feasibility check, after l,h from non-N constraints, compute union of excluded intervals; if they don't cover [l,h], feasible.
To recover δ, we can find first gap in [l,h] not covered. Use sorted intervals.
This is robust and vectorized for Q>0/0, and loop over negative constraints for exclusion intervals.

This generic line solver enables arbitrary sparse v.

Feasibility algorithm:
```
def line_check(T, base, L, Q, lo, hi):
    l=lo; h=hi; eps=1e-14
    # Q>=0
    nonneg = Q >= 0
    # For Q=0 handle linear
    z = nonneg & (Q == 0)
    if any z:
       Lz=L[z]; bz=base[z]
       zero = abs(Lz) <= 1e-15
       if any zero and bz[zero] > T+1e-12: return False
       pos = Lz > 1e-15
       if any pos: h = min(h, np.min((T-bz[pos])/Lz[pos]))
       neg = Lz < -1e-15
       if any neg: l = max(l, np.max((T-bz[neg])/Lz[neg]))
       if h < l - eps: return False
    # Q>0
    posq = Q > 0
    if any posq:
       Lp=L[posq]; bp=base[posq]; Qp=Q[posq]
       diff = Lp*Lp - 4*Qp*(bp - T)
       if any diff < -1e-12: return False
       sd=np.sqrt(np.maximum(diff,0))
       r1=(-Lp - sd)/(2*Qp); r2=(-Lp + sd)/(2*Qp)
       l = max(l, float(np.max(r1)))
       h = min(h, float(np.min(r2)))
       if h < l - eps: return False
    # Q<0 exclusions
    negq = Q < 0
    if any negq:
       Ln=L[negq]; Bn=base[negq]; An=-Q[negq]
       D = Ln*Ln - 4*An*(T - Bn)
       valid = D > 1e-12
       if any valid:
          sqrtD=np.sqrt(D[valid])
          r1=(-Ln[valid]-sqrtD)/(2*An[valid]); r2=(-Ln[valid]+sqrtD)/(2*An[valid])
          # excluded interval (r1,r2), but clip to [l,h]
          inter = [(max(l,r1), min(h,r2)) for ... if max(l,r1) < min(h,r2)-eps]
          # union and see if covers [l,h]
          if union covers [l,h] with eps: return False
    return True, maybe gap endpoint
```
But note: The Q<0 constraints individually have feasible δ≤r1 or δ≥r2 (closed roots). The excluded intervals are open (r1,r2). Covering by open intervals: for feasibility at endpoints, union of closed? If intervals just touch, there is point. We can use eps. To find gap, sort by start, merge intervals if next.start <= current.end + eps. Then check start > l+eps or end < h-eps etc. If [l,h] not fully covered, feasible. For recovery, choose a point in a gap: if l < first.start - eps choose (l+start)/2; if gap between; if last.end < h choose. If none, infeasible.

However for random v, Q at shifts may be positive or negative; number negative maybe half of O(s^2) if support small and distances unique? But Q array length 1399; many negative due diagonal and same sign. For s=20, at most diag plus same sign pairs distances (≤C(10,2)=45) negative? If we split support into plus/minus, negative only same sign pairs. Could still 45. Plus constraints many. The negative interval list 45, sort cheap.

Choosing v directions. This is key. We can use stochastic/subgradient.

Option 1: Random sparse feasible directions:
- K pairs (2K indices): choose K random pairs, assign +1 to one, -1 to other. Sum zero. Optionally orient to reduce current top shifts: for each pair from a top shift d, choose sign so L_d negative. - But if combine multiple random pairs, they may cancel; line search can choose δ positive/negative. Good.
- We can try K=1 (pair move), K=2,3,5,8, etc.
- Candidate pool each pass: e.g., 200 random sparse directions with K in {1,3,7,15}. For each, line solve. Accept if new max lower. This is like random line descent. It can improve and escape? Pure descent may stall.

Maybe the most robust algorithm:
- Start with initial.
- Run an iterative "stochastic line descent" using random sparse directions (pairs and multi-pairs) and exact line search. It will reduce C rapidly. Since each line search is exact along direction, it can make large steps. Number of directions can be large.
- To escape, use random directions with increasing support size and occasional larger random perturbations + line descent (or restarts). Pure descent from random directions might find lower local than coordinate.
- We can also do "tabu" or random direction not necessarily descent: line search returns best δ along v; if it doesn't improve, we can still move to the second-best? The line minimizes max along v; if current max at δ=0 is a local min along v, no move along that direction improves. Random directions eventually find one.

Choosing delta from intervals after final T:
- We can implement a function `line_delta` that recomputes l,h from nonneg constraints and excluded intervals at T (or T+small), then selects a point.
Simpler: After binary, do a local small grid around δ=0? Or use the final feasible set. Implement robust:
```
def choose_delta(T,...):
   do same nonneg constraints -> l,h
   if h<l return 0
   gather excluded intervals with D>eps
   sort/merge
   find a gap:
      start=l
      for [a,b] in intervals:
         if start + eps < a:
            return (start+a)/2
         start = max(start, b)
         if start >= h - eps: maybe return h? if gap after before?
      if start + eps < h: return (start+h)/2
      # if covered? return 0
```
But if excluded intervals include endpoints, we should pick root if feasible. Use eps. If no gap, return 0.
Potential issue: The objective may be minimized at boundary of feasible set (root) not inside gap; any feasible point has max≤T. Choosing midpoint okay. It might pick a δ far from 0 causing large h changes; but feasible. It may jump over basin, good. Could be volatile. We can restrict δ magnitude to e.g., 0.5? Line bound already. For stability, maybe choose feasible point closest to 0 if multiple gaps: if left gap around negative and right gap positive, choose with smaller abs. Our gap search from l to h might choose far. Feasible function top-level:
```
def line_feasible(T, lo, hi, Bz0, Lp0, Bp0, Ln0, Bn0, Lpp, Bpp, Qpp, Lnn, Bnn, Ann, eps=1e-12):
   l=lo; h=hi
   if len(Bz0)>0 and np.any(Bz0 > T + eps): return False
   if len(Lp0)>0:
      ub = np.min((T - Bp0) / Lp0)
      if ub < h: h = ub
   if len(Ln0)>0:
      lb = np.max((T - Bn0) / Ln0)  # Ln0 negative
      if lb > l: l = lb
   if h < l - eps: return False
   if len(Lpp)>0:
      diff = Lpp*Lpp - 4.0*Qpp*(Bpp - T)
      if np.any(diff < -1e-10): return False
      sd = np.sqrt(np.maximum(diff, 0.0))
      r1 = (-Lpp - sd) / (2.0*Qpp)
      r2 = (-Lpp + sd) / (2.0*Qpp)
      r1max = float(np.max(r1)); r2min=float(np.min(r2))
      if r1max > l: l=r1max
      if r2min < h: h=r2min
      if h < l - eps: return False
   if len(Lnn)>0:
      D = Lnn*Lnn - 4.0*Ann*(T - Bnn)
      # For each with D>eps, excluded interval roots clipped to [l,h]
      valid = D > 1e-12
      if np.any(valid):
         Lnv = Lnn[valid]; Bnv=Bnn[valid]; Anv=Ann[valid]; Dv=D[valid]
         sd = np.sqrt(Dv)
         r1 = (-Lnv - sd) / (2.0*Anv)
         r2 = (-Lnv + sd) / (2.0*Anv)
         a = np.maximum(r1, l); b = np.minimum(r2, h)
         keep = a < b - 1e-13
         a=a[keep]; b=b[keep]
         if len(a)>0:
            order=np.argsort(a)
            a=a[order]; b=b[order]
            # merge intervals and check coverage
            cov_start = l
            cur_a = a[0]; cur_b = b[0]
            # Actually check coverage from l to h. We'll merge intervals clipped.
            # If gap before first: if a[0] > l + eps -> feasible
            # then merge.
```
Need coverage check efficient.

def line_choose(T, pre, eps=1e-12):
   l,h = pre[0],pre[1]
   ... update l,h for nonneg as in feasible
   if h<l-eps: return 0.0

         i = rng.randint(0, n-d)
         pos=[i,i+d]
      elif d<0:
         e=-d; j=rng.randint(e,n); pos=[j-e,j]
      else:

            used.add(p); indices[cnt]=p; cnt+=1

      if attempts>10000: fill random

`add_deriv(p,val,L,h,n)` slices:
```
L[n-1] += val*(1.0 - 2.0*h[p])
# positive shifts: p first
end1 = 2*n - 1 - p
if end1 > n:
    L[n:end1] += val*(1.0 - h[p+1:])
# p second
if p > 0:
    L[n:n+p] += val*(-h[:p][::-1])
# negative shifts: p first
if p > 0:
    L[n-1-p:n-1] += val*(1.0 - h[:p][::-1])
# p second
end2 = n - 1
if p + 1 < n:
    L[p:end2] += val*(-h[p+1:][::-1])
```

- pos first: slice L[n : 2n-1-p] length (2n-1-p)-n = n-1-p. h[p+1:] length n-1-p. Good.
- pos second: L[n:n+p] length p. h[:p][::-1] length p.
- neg first: L[n-1-p:n-1] length p. h[:p][::-1] length p.
- neg second: L[p:n-1] length n-1-p. h[p+1:][::-1] length n-1-p.

`project_feasible` via λ: If we use line moves with sum exactly, h stays within bounds by domain. After many, maybe slight out; project. It uses sum target = n/2. If h already very close, no change. λ bisection:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    target=n/2.0
    s=h.sum()
    if abs(s-target)<1e-12: return np.clip(h,0,1)
    lo=-h.max(); hi=1.0-h.min()
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.clip(h+mid,0,1).sum()
       if s<target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    return np.clip(h+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
```
This Euclidean projection may change h more than needed but okay. For line moves sum already target, if small out of bounds due rounding, λ small.

But projecting after every accept may slightly perturb h along directions not sum-zero? Sum zero line preserves sum, so only bounds. If no out of bounds, λ=0. Good.

Pseudo:
```
def stochastic_lines(h, c, n, deadline, rng):
    dx=2.0/n
    best_h=h.copy(); best_cmax=c.max(); cur_h=h.copy(); cur_c=c.copy(); cur_cmax=best_cmax
    no_imp=0
    max_step=1.0
    iter_count=0
    # schedule max_step: for first 20% 1.0, then 0.5, then 0.2, at end 0.05
    while time.time()<deadline:
       remain = deadline-time.time()
       # direction type
       r=rng.random_sample()
       # k selection: use remainder? k = 1 if r2<0.4 elif 2<0.7 elif 4<0.9 else 8
       if r < 0.30: v=gradient_dir(n,cur_h,cur_c,max(1,min(n//2,...)), rng)
       elif r<0.65: v=biased_multi_dir(n,cur_c,k,rng)
       else: v=random_multi_dir(n,k,rng)
       step=max_step schedule based on no_imp or time
       delta = line_search(cur_h,cur_c,v,n,step)
       if abs(delta)>1e-12:
          # update cur_h
          for idx,val in v: cur_h[idx] += delta*val
          # due support sum zero; project
          cur_h = project_feasible(cur_h,n)
          newc = np.correlate(cur_h,1-cur_h,'full')
          newmax=newc.max()
          if newmax < cur_cmax - 1e-12:
             cur_cmax=newmax; cur_c=newc; no_imp=0
          elif newmax <= cur_cmax + 1e-10:
             # accept neutral? maybe if annealing temp
             # for simplicity accept if newmax < cur_cmax + 1e-9 and no_imp>500? could allow plateau
             accept = (newmax < cur_cmax + 1e-10)
             if accept:
                cur_cmax=newmax; cur_c=newc
             no_imp += 1
          else:
             no_imp += 1
             # maybe if newmax < best_cmax: update best but not cur? Actually cur worsened, don't.
          if newmax < best_cmax - 1e-12:
             best_cmax=newmax; best_h=cur_h.copy(); best_c=cur_c.copy(); no_imp=0
       else:
          no_imp += 1
       # if no improvements too long, kick
       if no_imp > 300 and remain>60:
          # kick cur from best with noise
          scale = 0.02 + 0.08*rng.random_sample()
          cur_h = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0, scale, n), n)
          cur_c = np.correlate(cur_h,1-cur_h,'full'); cur_cmax=cur_c.max()
          no_imp=0
          # if kicked worse than best, okay.
       # reduce max_step over time? use no_imp maybe
       # if no_imp very large and time low, skip kicks and just random small?
    return best_h, best_cmax
```
But if cur moves worse, best stored; no improvement increments. Kicks reset to best (or best+noise), so cur not stuck. Good.
We might accept worse moves without annealing. Kicks are random jumps from best, so not full simulated annealing but enough? It only jumps from best, not gradually. Could get stuck if random noise not helpful. We can also kick from current with noise. Maybe if current worse, kick from current? We can do `base = best_h` if rng<0.5 else cur_h`? If cur worsened, maybe perturb cur. But if no improvement from best, perturb best. We can try both.
   `kick_from = best_h if rng.random()<0.7 else cur_h`
   scale maybe 0.03-rng .15. Project. Then line descent. This can explore.

Direction k selection can be random. For gradient_dir, k maybe 2,4,8 (support 2k? Wait my gradient_dir returns support size 2k? Need define.    G=np.zeros(n)

       if d==0: ...
   raw=-G
   # select pos top k, neg bottom k
   order=np.argsort(raw)
   neg=order[:max(1,k)]
   pos=order[-max(1,k):][::-1]
   # build unique sets
```

`biased_multi_dir`:

def biased_multi_dir(n,c,k,rng):
   s=2*k
   top = np.argpartition(c, -min(20,n?))[-min(20,2*n-1):]
   used=set(); inds=np.empty(s,dtype=int); cnt=0; attempts=0
   while cnt<s and attempts<10*s:
      m=top[rng.randint(len(top))]
      d=m-(n-1)
      if d>0:
         i=rng.randint(0,n-d); a=i; b=i+d
      elif d<0:
         e=-d; j=rng.randint(e,n); a=j-e; b=j
      else:
         a=rng.randint(n); b=rng.randint(n-1); if b==a: b=(b+1)%n
      for p in (a,b):
         if p not in used:
            used.add(p); inds[cnt]=p; cnt+=1
            if cnt==s: break
      attempts+=1
   if cnt<s:
      rem=[]
      while len(rem)<s-cnt:
          p=rng.randint(n)
          if p not in used: used.add(p); rem.append(p)
      inds[cnt:]=rem; cnt=s
   vals=np.empty(s); vals[:s//2]=1; vals[s//2:]=-1; rng.shuffle(vals)
   return list(zip(inds.tolist(), vals.tolist()))
```
Potential expensive set operations for many calls. But s small; okay. Top argpartition each call O(M log k) ~1400; 100k calls => heavy. We can compute top shifts outside and pass? Simpler: compute inside but expensive. We can design `biased_multi_dir` to receive precomputed top indices. Main loop updates `top_idx` after every accept or every 20 iterations.

Implement `top_indices(c, size)`:
```
def top_indices(c, size):
   m=len(c)
   if size>=m: return np.arange(m)
   return np.argpartition(c, -size)[-size:]
```

def quick_project(h,n):
   h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)

So implement `fast_project(h,n,target)` and `project_feasible` for large changes. Fast:
```
def fast_project(h,n):
   target=n*0.5
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<=1e-12: return h
   h = h + diff/n
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)>1e-11:
      # iterative up to 20 using interior delta
      for _ in range(20):
         mask=(h>1e-14)&(h<1-1e-14)
         cnt=mask.sum()
         if cnt==0: break
         delta=diff/cnt
         h[mask]=np.clip(h[mask]+delta,0,1)
         diff=target-h.sum()
         if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
   if abs(diff)>1e-8:
      h=project_feasible(h,n)
   return h
```
Sum() each loop O(n). For line accepts, diff tiny, one loop. Good.

def stochastic_lines(h,c,n,deadline,rng):
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=eval_c5(h,n)
    cur_h=h.copy(); cur_c=c.copy(); cur_cmax=float(cur_c.max())
    dx=2.0/n
    top_size=min(70, 2*n-1)
    topc=top_indices(cur_c, top_size)
    no_imp=0; accept=0; attempts=0
    while time.time()<deadline:
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       # direction
       r1=rng.random_sample(); r2=rng.random_sample()
       kmax=min(8, n//20 if n>40 else 1)
       if r2 <0.5: k=1
       elif r2<0.8: k=2
       elif r2<0.95: k=min(4,kmax)
       else: k=max(1,kmax)

          v=gradient_dir(n,cur_h,cur_c,topc,kk,rng)
       elif r1 <0.70:
          v=biased_multi_dir(n,cur_c,topc,k,rng)
       else:
          v=random_multi_dir(n,k,rng)
       frac=(time.time()-t0? Can't access t0? Pass deadline and t0? We can pass t0.)
```

Code skeleton:
```python
import numpy as np, time

def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    target=0.5*n
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    if abs(h.sum()-target)<1e-12: return h
    lo=-float(h.max()); hi=1.0-float(h.min())
    for _ in range(70):
       md=(lo+hi)*0.5
       s=float(np.clip(h+md,0,1).sum())
       if s<target: lo=md
       else: hi=md
    return np.clip(h+(lo+hi)*0.5,0,1)

def eval_c(h,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')))*dx
```
`fast project` maybe not needed if line updates sum zero. For kicks use project_feasible. For final use project_feasible.

`add_deriv`:
```python
def add_deriv(p,val,L,h,n):
    L[n-1] += val*(1.0-2.0*h[p])
    e=2*n-1-p
    if e>n:
        L[n:e] += val*(1.0-h[p+1:])
    if p>0:
        L[n:n+p] += val*(-h[:p][::-1])
    if p>0:
        L[n-1-p:n-1] += val*(1.0-h[:p][::-1])
    if p+1<n:
        L[p:n-1] += val*(-h[p+1:][::-1])
```
Check if slice length zero: `if p+1 < n: L[p:n-1] += ...` includes p to n-2. For p=n-1, p+1<n false. For p=0, L[0:n-1] length n-1. Good.

`make_pre`:
```python
def make_pre(base,L,Q,lo,hi):
    m=Q==0.0
    if m.any():
        Lz=L[m]; Bz=base[m]
        pos=Lz>1e-14; neg=Lz<-1e-14; zero=~(pos|neg)
        Lp=Lz[pos]; Bp=Bz[pos]; Ln=Lz[neg]; Bn=Bz[neg]; Bz0=Bz[zero]
    else: arrays empty
    mp=Q>1e-14 ...
    mn=Q<-1e-14 ... Ann=-Q[mn]
    return [lo,hi,Bz0,Lp,Bp,Ln,Bn,Lq,Bq,Qq,Ln2,Bn2,An2]
```
Need using same name `Lp` for linear and `Lq`; okay.

`line_check` as above. Let's write compact:
```python
def line_check(T,pre,want):
    eps=1e-11; ex=1e-10
    l=pre[0]; h=pre[1]
    Bz=pre[2]; Lp=pre[3]; Bp=pre[4]; Ln=pre[5]; Bn=pre[6]
    if Bz.size and np.any(Bz > T+eps): return False, None
    if Lp.size:
        ub=float(np.min((T-Bp)/Lp)); h=min(h,ub)
    if Ln.size:
        lb=float(np.max((T-Bn)/Ln)); l=max(l,lb)
    if h<l-eps: return False, None
    Lq=pre[7]; Bq=pre[8]; Qq=pre[9]
    if Lq.size:
        diff=Lq*Lq - 4.0*Qq*(Bq-T)
        if np.any(diff < -1e-10): return False, None
        sd=np.sqrt(np.maximum(diff,0.0))
        r1=(-Lq-sd)/(2.0*Qq); r2=(-Lq+sd)/(2.0*Qq)
        l=max(l,float(np.max(r1))); h=min(h,float(np.min(r2)))
        if h<l-eps: return False, None
    Ln2=pre[10]; Bn2=pre[11]; An2=pre[12]
    if Ln2.size:
        D=Ln2*Ln2 - 4.0*An2*(T-Bn2)
        valid=D>1e-10
        if np.any(valid):
            Lnv=Ln2[valid]; Bnv=Bn2[valid]; Anv=An2[valid]; Dv=D[valid]
            sd=np.sqrt(Dv)
            r1=(-Lnv-sd)/(2.0*Anv); r2=(-Lnv+sd)/(2.0*Anv)
            a=np.maximum(r1+ex,l); b=np.minimum(r2-ex,h)
            keep=a < b - 1e-13
            if np.any(keep):
                a=a[keep]; b=b[keep]
                ord_ = np.argsort(a)
                a=a[ord_]; b=b[ord_]
                last=l
                best=None; bestd=1e100
                for i in range(a.size):
                    s=a[i]; e=b[i]
                    if s > last + ex:
                        if last <= 0.0 <= s:
                            if not want: return True,None
                            return True,0.0
                        d0 = 0.0 if last <=0<=s else abs(s) if s>0 else abs(last)
                        if want and d0 < bestd:
                            bestd=d0; best=(last,s)
                        if not want:
                            return True,None
                    if e > last: last=e
                    if last >= h - ex:
                        break
                if not want:
                    return (last < h - ex), None
                if last < h - ex:
                    if last <= 0.0 <= h:
                        return True,0.0
                    d0 = abs(h) if h>0 else abs(last)
                    if d0 < bestd: best=(last,h)
                if best is None:
                    return False, None
                a,b=best
                pt=0.0
                if a>0: pt=a
                elif b<0: pt=b
                else: pt=0.0
                return True, float(pt)
            # if no intervals after keep, fall through feasible
    if want:
        pt=0.0
        if l>0: pt=l
        elif h<0: pt=h
        return True, pt
    return True, None
```
Need ensure `last>=h-ex break` without checking gap after? If last reaches h, no more gaps; if want and no best found, return False. If not want, break then after loop returns last<h? false. Good.
If first interval starts after l, in loop before updating last: s>last; returns if not want or records gap. Good.
If interval begins before l? a clipped to max(l,r1+ex) so >=l. If a==l, no gap before.
If an interval entirely outside [l,h], keep false (a>=b-...), so no exclusion.
If multiple intervals, gap recording. For want, if 0 in later gap return immediately. Good.

One subtlety: For concave constraints, roots r1,r2 may be such that r1>r2? formula r1=(-L - sqrtD)/(2A), r2=(-L + sqrtD)/(2A), A>0 => r1≤r2. Good.

`line_search`:
```python
def line_search(h,c,v,n,max_step):
    s=len(v)
    if s==0: return 0.0,c,float(c.max())
    lo=-max_step; hi=max_step
    vals=[]
    for idx,val in v:
       hv=float(h[idx])
       if val>0:
          lo=max(lo, -hv/val); hi=min(hi,(1-hv)/val)
       else:
          lo=max(lo,(1-hv)/val); hi=min(hi,-hv/val)
    if hi < lo + 1e-13: return 0.0,c,float(c.max())
    M=2*n-1
    L=np.zeros(M)
    for idx,val in v: add_deriv(idx,float(val),L,h,n)
    Q=np.zeros(M)
    sv=0.0
    vidx=[]; vval=[]
    for idx,val in v:
       vidx.append(idx); vval.append(float(val)); sv += val*val
    Q[n-1] = -sv
    for a in range(s):
       ia=vidx[a]; va=vval[a]
       for b in range(a+1,s):
          ib=vidx[b]; vb=vval[b]
          d=ia-ib
          if d>0:
             Q[n-1+d] += -va*vb
             Q[n-1-d] += -va*vb
          elif d<0:
             Q[n-1+d] += -va*vb
             Q[n-1-d] += -va*vb
    pre=make_pre(c,L,Q,lo,hi)
    curmax=float(c.max())
    ok,_=line_check(curmax,pre,False)
    if not ok: curmax += 1e-8
    low=0.0; high=curmax
    for _ in range(32):
        mid=(low+high)*0.5
        ok,_=line_check(mid,pre,False)
        if ok: high=mid
        else: low=mid
    ok,delta=line_check(high,pre,True)
    if not ok:
        # try high+ small
        ok,delta=line_check(high+1e-8,pre,True)
        if not ok:
            return 0.0,c,curmax
        high += 1e-8
    if delta is None: delta=0.0
    delta=float(delta)
    if abs(delta)<1e-13:
        return 0.0,c,curmax
    new_c=c + L*delta + Q*delta*delta
    new_c=np.maximum(new_c,0.0)  # maybe avoid tiny neg? Could affect max? Max unaffected by tiny neg. But if c max near 0? okay. However new_c exact formula might have tiny > true; clipping negative fine.
    return delta,new_c,float(new_c.max())
```
For d difference, if d=0 skip duplicates. If v has duplicate indices? We ensure no duplicates; but if duplicates, Q diagonal sv? Off pairs with same idx d=0 would add to Q[n-1]? not. We should ensure directions unique. Our dir functions use unique. Good.

`np.maximum(new_c,0)` might hide negative but c should be nonnegative. Fine. But for future incremental, if we clip negative to 0, c slightly increased (true maybe negative? impossible). okay.

Potential issue: In line_search, Q off-diag addition: For d positive, `Q[n-1+d]` and `Q[n-1-d]`. If d=0 skip. If multiple pairs same distance, sums. Good. For d maybe >n-1? indices within 0..n-1 so |d|<n. Good.

`top_indices`:
```python
def top_indices(c,size):
    m=c.size
    if size<=0: return np.empty(0,int)
    if size>=m: return np.arange(m)
    return np.argpartition(c, -size)[-size:]
```

`random_multi_dir`:
```python
def random_multi_dir(n,k,rng):
    s=max(2,2*k)
    if k<=0: k=1
    if s>n: s=n if n%2==0 else n-1
    ind=rng.choice(n, s, replace=False)
    vals=np.empty(s); vals[s//2:]=-1.0; vals[:s//2]=1.0; rng.shuffle(vals)
    return list(zip(ind.tolist(), vals.tolist()))
```
If s odd? s even. If n odd and s=n-1 maybe even. Ensure if s<2 return empty? Use k=1 s=2, if n<2? n>=50? fallback n=500.

`biased_multi_dir` with unique indices:
```python
def biased_multi_dir(n,c,topc,k,rng):
    s=max(2,2*k)
    if len(topc)==0: return random_multi_dir(n,k,rng)
    used=set(); inds=list()
    tries=0
    while len(inds)<s and tries<20*s:
        m=int(topc[rng.randint(len(topc))])
        d=m-(n-1)
        if d>0:
           i=rng.randint(0,n-d); a=i; b=i+d
        elif d<0:
           e=-d; j=rng.randint(e,n); a=j-e; b=j
        else:
           a=rng.randint(n); b=rng.randint(n-1)
           if b==a: b=(b+1)%n
        for p in (a,b):
           if p not in used:
              used.add(p); inds.append(p)
              if len(inds)==s: break
        tries+=1
    if len(inds)<s:
        while len(inds)<s:
           p=int(rng.randint(n))
           if p not in used:
              used.add(p); inds.append(p)
    inds=np.array(inds[:s],dtype=int)
    vals=np.empty(s); vals[s//2:]=-1; vals[:s//2]=1; rng.shuffle(vals)
    return list(zip(inds.tolist(), vals.tolist()))
```
If topc contains m such that d=0 and n=1? fine.
Sum vals zero if s even and half each. If s changed due n odd? s=2k even. If n small and s>n? For n<2? fallback. If s>n and n odd, can't equal counts; ignore since n large.

`gradient_dir`:
```python
def gradient_dir(n,h,c,topc,k,rng):
    k=max(1,min(k,n//2))
    act=min(40,len(topc))
    if act<=0: return random_multi_dir(n,k,rng)
    idx=topc[:act]
    cv=c[idx]
    temp=max(1e-6,0.02*(float(cv.max())-float(cv.min()))+1e-6)
    w=np.exp((cv-cv.max())/temp); w=w/float(w.sum())
    G=np.zeros(n)
    for mi,wi in zip(idx,w):
       mi=int(mi); wi=float(wi)
       d=mi-(n-1)
       if d==0:
          G += wi*(1.0-2.0*h)
       elif d>0:
          G[:n-d] += wi*(1.0-h[d:])
          G[d:] += -wi*h[:-d]
       else:
          e=-d
          G[e:] += wi*(1.0-h[:-e])
          G[:n-e] += -wi*h[e:]
    raw=-G
    mu=float(raw.mean())
    raw=raw-mu
    if not np.any(np.abs(raw)>1e-14): return random_multi_dir(n,k,rng)
    order=np.argsort(raw)
    neg=order[:k]
    pos=order[-k:][::-1]
    used=set(); res=[]
    for i in pos:
       i=int(i)
       if i not in used:
          used.add(i); res.append((i,1.0))
    for i in neg:
       i=int(i)
       if i not in used:
          used.add(i); res.append((i,-1.0))
    target=2*k
    poscnt=len([1 for _,v in res if v>0]); negcnt=len([1 for _,v in res if v<0])
    while len(res)<target:
       i=int(rng.randint(n))
       if i in used: continue
       if poscnt<negcnt: val=1.0; poscnt+=1
       elif negcnt<poscnt: val=-1.0; negcnt+=1
       else:
          val=1.0 if rng.random_sample()<0.5 else -1.0
          if val>0: poscnt+=1
          else: negcnt+=1
       used.add(i); res.append((i,val))
    if len(res)>target:
       # truncating might break sum; instead reverse? target reached in loop, no >.
```

def resample_h(h,n_new):
    n_old=len(h)
    x_old=np.linspace(0,2,n_old,endpoint=False)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,2,n_new,endpoint=False)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x_new,x_old,h),n_new)
```

`stochastic_lines` uses top_indices each 50 attempts; topc initially. It needs `dx` and returns C. Let's write final version carefully.

```
def stochastic_lines(h,c,n,deadline,rng,t0,budget_s):
    dx=2.0/n
    h=project_feasible(h,n) # ensures
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    curC=float(c.max())*dx
    best_h=h.copy(); bestC=curC
    cur_h=h; cur_c=c
    topc=top_indices(cur_c, min(70,2*n-1))
    no_imp=0; accept=0; att=0
    nkm=max(1,min(8,n//10))
    while time.time()<deadline:
       frac=min(1.0,(time.time()-t0)/max(1.0,budget_s))
       if frac<0.30: st=1.0
       elif frac<0.65: st=0.5
       elif frac<0.90: st=0.15
       else: st=0.04
       r1=rng.random_sample(); r2=rng.random_sample()
       if r2<0.50: k=1
       elif r2<0.80: k=2
       elif r2<0.95: k=4
       else: k=nkm
       k=max(1,min(k,n//2 if n>2 else 1))
       if r1<0.30:
           v=gradient_dir(n,cur_h,cur_c,topc,max(2,k),rng)
       elif r1<0.70:
           v=biased_multi_dir(n,cur_c,topc,k,rng)
       else:
           v=random_multi_dir(n,k,rng)
       delta,new_c,new_cmax=line_search(cur_h,cur_c,v,n,st)
       newC=new_cmax*dx
       if abs(delta)>1e-12 and newC < curC - 1e-11:
           # apply
           for idx,val in v:
               nv=cur_h[idx]+delta*val
               if nv<0.0: nv=0.0
               elif nv>1.0: nv=1.0
               cur_h[idx]=nv
           cur_c=new_c
           curC=newC
           accept +=1
           if newC < bestC - 1e-12:
               bestC=newC; best_h=cur_h.copy()
           no_imp=0
           if accept % 80 ==0:
               cur_c=np.correlate(cur_h,1-cur_h,'full')
               curC=float(cur_c.max())*dx
               if curC < bestC - 1e-12:
                   bestC=curC; best_h=cur_h.copy()
       else:
           no_imp +=1
       att +=1
       if att % 60 ==0:
           topc=top_indices(cur_c, min(70,2*n-1))
       if no_imp > 400 and time.time() > deadline - 60:
           # still kick? only if enough time
           base = best_h if rng.random_sample()<0.7 else cur_h
           scale=0.02+0.12*rng.random_sample()
           cur_h=project_feasible(base + rng.normal(0,scale,n),n)
           cur_c=np.correlate(cur_h,1-cur_h,'full')
           curC=float(cur_c.max())*dx
           topc=top_indices(cur_c, min(70,2*n-1))
           no_imp=0
           if curC < bestC - 1e-12:
               bestC=curC; best_h=cur_h.copy()
    # final recompute best exact
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    bc=float(np.max(np.correlate(best_h,1-best_h,'full')))*dx
    if bc < bestC: bc=bc
    # if best_h was approximate and recompute worse, maybe cur_h recompute better?
    cc=float(np.max(np.correlate(project_feasible(cur_h,n),1-...)))? Could update.
    return best_h,bc
```
In final, ensure best_h feasible. If bc > bestC approximate, set bestC=bc. But we need best_h corresponds to bestC. Use `h_check=best_h; c_check=...; if c_check < bestC?`; If c_check > bestC, bestC=c_check. Also check cur_h exact; if better update.

Now, let's consider mathematical correctness of line feasibility for Q>0 and Q<0 with base/L/Q arrays. It minimizes `max_m (base_m + L_m δ + Q_m δ^2)` subject to δ bounds. The feasibility check for T requires all constraints ≤T. For Q>0, each is interval; intersection connected. For linear, connected. For Q<0, each is complement of interval; we intersect [l,h] with all complements by union of excluded intervals. We clipped exclusions to [l,h] after nonneg constraints. Is it valid to first intersect nonneg to [l,h], then check [l,h] not covered by union of exclusion intervals from Q<0? Yes, since Q<0 constraints independent; feasible if exists δ in [l,h] not in any excluded interval. Union coverage check correct.

But what about Q<0 constraints with D<=0: inequality all δ feasible. We skip. If D>0 but roots outside [l,h], after clipping keep false (no exclusion). Good.

`line_check` for Q>0 uses `if np.any(diff < -1e-10): return False`. If one constraint has diff slightly negative due numerical but true min at T, could false. T from binary might need small tolerance. We could set tolerance -1e-9. Use -1e-10 okay. To be safer, if diff < 0 but > -1e-9 treat as 0. In code, `if np.any(diff < -1e-9): return False`. Then `sd=sqrt(max(diff,0))`. Use same for negative D? For Q<0 D validity >1e-10; if D slightly negative, no exclusion. Good.

In `line_check`, for zero linear Bz: if Bz > T+eps infeasible. eps=1e-11 maybe too small vs c up to 140; use `tol=1e-10`. If T is binary, tolerance allowing high slightly below true min by 1e-10 c (C 1e-13), okay. Use eps=1e-10.

The line_search binary 32 iterations; high final might be low by eps. Good.

Let's check if `line_search` initial high `curmax` feasible if `new_c` has small negative? high=max(c). For T=curmax, all base≤T. But some Q>0 constraints with B=T and L nonzero: interval includes δ=0? diff=L^2, roots [-L/2-|L|/2, -L/2+|L|/2] includes 0? If L=1: roots -1,0; yes 0 at endpoint. Our Q>0 feasibility at T high: diff=L^2, r1=-L-|L| /2, r2=-L+|L|/2. If L>0, r2=0; h=min(h,0). If l maybe -st, hi=0. Feasible if l≤0. δ=0 allowed. But due eps, r2=0, hi=0. If Q<0 diag, at T high, exclusion? c0 diagonal concave: at δ=0 base maybe T; inequality -Q? For Q=-s, D=L^2 -4s(T-B). If B=T, D=L^2. Roots around? Excluded interior where c>T. δ=0 root if L=0? If L nonzero, 0 may be inside excluded? For a single concave, at δ=0 c=T, so should be feasible at root; excluded open interval not include 0 if 0 is root. But if base not T for that constraint? - as first i=j if j<=n-d-1: 1-h_{j+d}
- as second i=j-d if j>=d: -h_{j-d}
Our code: `G[:n-d] += wi*(1-h[d:])` (j=0..n-d-1) correct. `G[d:] += -wi*h[:-d]` (j=d..n-1) correct.
For d<0, e=-d: c_{-e}=∑_{i=e}^{n-1} h_i(1-h_{i-e}). Gradient:
- as first i=j if j>=e: 1-h_{j-e}
- as second j=i-e if i=j+e: -h_{j+e} for j=0..n-e-1.
Code: `G[e:] += wi*(1-h[:-e])` (j=e..n-1, values h[j-e]=h[0..]) correct. `G[:n-e] += -wi*h[e:]` (j=0..n-e-1) correct.

Revised `make_pre` compact:
```python
def make_pre(base,L,Q,lo,hi):
 e=np.empty(0)
 m=(Q==0.0)
 if m.any():
  Lz=L[m];Bz=base[m];p=Lz>1e-14;q=Lz<-1e-14;z=~(p|q)
  Lp=Lz[p];Bp=Bz[p];Ln=Lz[q];Bn=Bz[q];Bz0=Bz[z]
 else: Lp=Bp=Ln=Bn=Bz0=e
 m=Q>1e-14
 if m.any(): Lq=L[m];Bq=base[m];Qq=Q[m]
 else: Lq=Bq=Qq=e
 m=Q<-1e-14
 if m.any(): Ln2=L[m];Bn2=base[m];An2=-Q[m]
 else: Ln2=Bn2=An2=e
 return [lo,hi,Bz0,Lp,Bp,Ln,Bn,Lq,Bq,Qq,Ln2,Bn2,An2]
```
Good.

import numpy as np, time

def project_feasible(h,n):
 h=np.clip(np.array(h,dtype=float).copy(),0,1); t=0.5*n
 if abs(h.sum()-t)<1e-12: return h
 lo=-float(h.max()); hi=1-float(h.min())
 for _ in range(60):
  m=(lo+hi)*.5; s=float(np.clip(h+m,0,1).sum())
  if s<t: lo=m
  else: hi=m
 return np.clip(h+(lo+hi)*.5,0,1)

def resample_h(h,n2):

Let's detail `line_check` code exactly:
```
def line_check(T, pre, want):
    eps=1e-10; ex=1e-10
    l=pre[0]; h=pre[1]
    Bz=pre[2]; Lp=pre[3]; Bp=pre[4]; Ln=pre[5]; Bn=pre[6]
    if Bz.size and np.any(Bz > T + eps): return False, None
    if Lp.size:
        ub=float(np.min((T-Bp)/Lp)); 
        if ub < h: h=ub
    if Ln.size:
        lb=float(np.max((T-Bn)/Ln))
        if lb > l: l=lb
    if h < l - eps: return False, None
    Lq=pre[7]; Bq=pre[8]; Qq=pre[9]
    if Lq.size:
        diff=Lq*Lq - 4.0*Qq*(Bq - T)
        if np.any(diff < -1e-9): return False, None
        sd=np.sqrt(np.maximum(diff,0.0))
        r1=(-Lq-sd)/(2.0*Qq); r2=(-Lq+sd)/(2.0*Qq)
        r1m=float(np.max(r1)); r2m=float(np.min(r2))
        if r1m > l: l=r1m
        if r2m < h: h=r2m
        if h < l - eps: return False, None
    Ln2=pre[10]; Bn2=pre[11]; An2=pre[12]
    if Ln2.size:
       D=Ln2*Ln2 - 4.0*An2*(T - Bn2)
       valid=D > 1e-9
       if np.any(valid):
          Lnv=Ln2[valid]; Anv=An2[valid]; Dv=D[valid]
          sd=np.sqrt(Dv)
          r1=(-Lnv-sd)/(2.0*Anv); r2=(-Lnv+sd)/(2.0*Anv)
          a=np.maximum(r1+ex,l); b=np.minimum(r2-ex,h)
          keep=a < b - 1e-13
          if np.any(keep):
             a=a[keep]; b=b[keep]
             o=np.argsort(a); a=a[o]; b=b[o]
             last=l; best=None; bestd=1e100
             for i in range(a.size):
                s=a[i]; e=b[i]
                if s > last + ex:
                   if last <= 0.0 <= s:
                      if not want: return True, None
                      return True, 0.0
                   d0=abs(s) if s > 0.0 else abs(last)
                   if want and d0 < bestd:
                      bestd=d0; best=(last,s)
                   if not want: return True, None
                if e > last: last=e
                if last >= h - ex: break
             if not want:
                return (last < h - ex), None
             if last < h - ex:
                if last <= 0.0 <= h:
                   return True, 0.0
                d0=abs(h) if h > 0.0 else abs(last)
                if d0 < bestd: best=(last,h)
             if best is None: return False, None
             aa,bb=best
             if aa > 0.0: return True, float(aa)
             if bb < 0.0: return True, float(bb)
             return True, 0.0
             # Actually if aa<=0<=bb, return 0; else endpoints.
    if want:
       if l > 0.0: return True, float(l)
       if h < 0.0: return True, float(h)
    return (not want) or True, None if not want else 0.0
```
The last return messy. Let's simplify:
```
    if want:
       if l > 0: return True, float(l)
       if h < 0: return True, float(h)
       return True, 0.0
    return True, None
```
But if `best` found with gap not containing 0, and endpoint should be chosen. In above, after loop if best None? If best not None, choose. Code:
```
            if best is None:
                return False, None
            aa,bb=best
            if aa <= 0.0 <= bb: return True, 0.0
            if aa > 0.0: return True, float(aa)
            return True, float(bb)
    if want: ...
```
If no negative intervals or no keep, falls to final; if 0 not within [l,h], choose endpoint. Good.

For not want, if break due last>=h, returns last<h false. Good.

One flaw: For arbitrary v, c_m(δ) = base + Lδ + Qδ². Is Q = -correlate(v,v) for all m? For full c, each off-diag term -h_i h_j with i≠j. Along h+δv, bilinear term changes by -2δ v_i v_j - δ² v_i v_j? Wait earlier we got coefficient +? Let's rederive general:
Term in c_d for d>0: -h_i h_j, with h_i=a+δv_i, h_j=b+δv_j. Old -a b. New -[ab + δ(v_i b + a v_j)+δ² v_i v_j]. Change = -δ(v_i b + a v_j) - δ² v_i v_j. The derivative part L includes -v_i b - a v_j; derivative formula `add_deriv` should produce that. Q coefficient for this pair is -v_i v_j. For d=0 term -h_i²: change -δ² v_i². Sum Q=-∑_pairs v_i v_j -∑ v_i² = -full autocorr. Yes. Our Q positive for opposite signs because -v_i v_j positive. For pair v=(1,-1), Q=+1. Correct.
For multi, diagonal Q=-s. Good.
L via add_deriv: derivative of -h_i h_j wrt h_i = -h_j, wrt h_j=-h_i. So L from val weights = -b v_i - a v_j. Good. And linear terms ∑h_i derivative included in add_deriv (1-... and -...). Good. Exactly.

```python
import numpy as np
import time

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    t = 0.5 * n
    if abs(h.sum() - t) < 1e-12:
        return h
    lo = -float(h.max())
    hi = 1.0 - float(h.min())
    for _ in range(60):
        m = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.clip(h + m, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if s < t:
            lo = m
        else:
            hi = m
    return np.clip(h + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)

def add_deriv(p, val, L, h, n):
    L[n - 1] += val * (1.0 - 2.0 * h[p])
    e = 2 * n - 1 - p
    if e > n:
        L[n:e] += val * (1.0 - h[p + 1:])
    if p > 0:
        L[n:n + p] += val * (-h[:p][::-1])
        L[n - 1 - p:n - 1] += val * (1.0 - h[:p][::-1])
    if p + 1 < n:
        L[p:n - 1] += val * (-h[p + 1:][::-1])

def make_pre(base, L, Q, lo, hi):
    e = np.empty(0)
    m = Q == 0.0
    if m.any():
        Lz = L[m]; Bz = base[m]
        p = Lz > 1e-14; q = Lz < -1e-14; z = ~(p | q)
        Lp = Lz[p]; Bp = Bz[p]; Ln = Lz[q]; Bn = Bz[q]; Bz0 = Bz[z]
    else:
        Lp = Bp = Ln = Bn = Bz0 = e
    m = Q > 1e-14
    if m.any():
        Lq = L[m]; Bq = base[m]; Qq = Q[m]
    else:
        Lq = Bq = Qq = e
    m = Q < -1e-14
    if m.any():
        Ln2 = L[m]; Bn2 = base[m]; An2 = -Q[m]
    else:
        Ln2 = Bn2 = An2 = e
    return [lo, hi, Bz0, Lp, Bp, Ln, Bn, Lq, Bq, Qq, Ln2, Bn2, An2]

def line_check(T, pre, want):
    eps = 1e-10
    ex = 1e-10
    l = pre[0]; h = pre[1]
    Bz = pre[2]; Lp = pre[3]; Bp = pre[4]; Ln = pre[5]; Bn = pre[6]
    if Bz.size and np.any(Bz > T + eps):
        return False, None
    if Lp.size:
        ub = float(np.min((T - Bp) / Lp))
        if ub < h:
            h = ub
    if Ln.size:
        lb = float(np.max((T - Bn) / Ln))
        if lb > l:
            l = lb
    if h < l - eps:
        return False, None
    Lq = pre[7]; Bq = pre[8]; Qq = pre[9]
    if Lq.size:
        diff = Lq * Lq - 4.0 * Qq * (Bq - T)
        if np.any(diff < -1e-9):
            return False, None
        sd = np.sqrt(np.maximum(diff, 0.0))
        r1 = (-Lq - sd) / (2.0 * Qq)
        r2 = (-Lq + sd) / (2.0 * Qq)
        if float(np.max(r1)) > l:
            l = float(np.max(r1))
        if float(np.min(r2)) < h:
            h = float(np.min(r2))
        if h < l - eps:
            return False, None
    Ln2 = pre[10]; Bn2 = pre[11]; An2 = pre[12]
    if Ln2.size:
        D = Ln2 * Ln2 - 4.0 * An2 * (T - Bn2)
        valid = D > 1e-9
        if np.any(valid):
            Lnv = Ln2[valid]; Anv = An2[valid]; Dv = D[valid]
            sd = np.sqrt(Dv)
            r1 = (-Lnv - sd) / (2.0 * Anv)
            r2 = (-Lnv + sd) / (2.0 * Anv)
            a = np.maximum(r1 + ex, l)
            b = np.minimum(r2 - ex, h)
            keep = a < b - 1e-13
            if np.any(keep):
                a = a[keep]; b = b[keep]
                o = np.argsort(a)
                a = a[o]; b = b[o]
                last = l
                best = None
                bestd = 1e100
                for ii in range(a.size):
                    s = a[ii]; e = b[ii]
                    if s > last + ex:
                        if last <= 0.0 <= s:
                            if not want:
                                return True, None
                            return True, 0.0
                        d0 = abs(s) if s > 0.0 else abs(last)
                        if want and d0 < bestd:
                            bestd = d0
                            best = (last, s)
                        if not want:
                            return True, None
                    if e > last:
                        last = e
                    if last >= h - ex:
                        break
                if not want:
                    return (last < h - ex), None
                if last < h - ex:
                    if last <= 0.0 <= h:
                        return True, 0.0
                    d0 = abs(h) if h > 0.0 else abs(last)
                    if d0 < bestd:
                        best = (last, h)
                if best is None:
                    return False, None
                aa, bb = best
                if aa <= 0.0 <= bb:
                    return True, 0.0
                if aa > 0.0:
                    return True, float(aa)
                return True, float(bb)
    if want:
        if l > 0.0:
            return True, float(l)
        if h < 0.0:
            return True, float(h)
        return True, 0.0
    return True, None

def line_search(h, c, v, n, max_step):
    s = len(v)
    if s < 2:
        return 0.0, c, float(c.max())
    lo = -max_step
    hi = max_step
    idxs = []
    vals = []
    sv = 0.0
    for idx, val in v:
        idx = int(idx)
        val = float(val)
        idxs.append(idx)
        vals.append(val)
        sv += val * val
        hv = float(h[idx])
        if val > 0.0:
            lo = max(lo, -hv / val)
            hi = min(hi, (1.0 - hv) / val)
        else:
            lo = max(lo, (1.0 - hv) / val)
            hi = min(hi, -hv / val)
    if hi < lo + 1e-13:
        return 0.0, c, float(c.max())
    M = 2 * n - 1
    L = np.zeros(M)
    for idx, val in zip(idxs, vals):
        add_deriv(idx, val, L, h, n)
    Q = np.zeros(M)
    Q[n - 1] = -sv
    for a in range(s):
        ia = idxs[a]
        va = vals[a]
        for b in range(a + 1, s):
            d = ia - idxs[b]
            if d:
                q = -va * vals[b]
                Q[n - 1 + d] += q
                Q[n - 1 - d] += q
    pre = make_pre(c, L, Q, lo, hi)
    cur = float(c.max())
    ok, _ = line_check(cur, pre, False)
    if not ok:
        cur += 1e-8
    low = 0.0
    high = cur
    for _ in range(32):
        mid = 0.5 * (low + high)
        ok, _ = line_check(mid, pre, False)
        if ok:
            high = mid
        else:
            low = mid
    ok, delta = line_check(high, pre, True)
    if not ok:
        ok, delta = line_check(high + 1e-8, pre, True)
        if not ok:
            return 0.0, c, float(c.max())
    delta = float(delta)
    if abs(delta) < 1e-13:
        return 0.0, c, float(c.max())
    new_c = c + L * delta + Q * delta * delta
    new_c = np.maximum(new_c, 0.0)
    return delta, new_c, float(new_c.max())

def top_indices(c, size):
    m = c.size
    if size <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=int)
    if size >= m:
        return np.arange(m)
    return np.argpartition(c, -size)[-size:]

def random_multi_dir(n, k, rng):
    k = max(1, int(k))
    s = 2 * k
    if s > n:
        s = n if n % 2 == 0 else n - 1
    if s < 2:
        return []
    ind = rng.choice(n, s, replace=False)
    vals = np.empty(s)
    vals[:s // 2] = 1.0
    vals[s // 2:] = -1.0
    rng.shuffle(vals)
    return list(zip(ind.tolist(), vals.tolist()))

def biased_multi_dir(n, c, topc, k, rng):
    k = max(1, int(k))
    s = 2 * k
    if s > n or len(topc) == 0:
        return random_multi_dir(n, k, rng)
    inds = []
    used = set()
    tries = 0
    while len(inds) < s and tries < 30 * s:
        m = int(topc[rng.randint(len(topc))])
        d = m - (n - 1)
        if d > 0:
            i = rng.randint(0, n - d)
            a, b = i, i + d
        elif d < 0:
            e = -d
            j = rng.randint(e, n)
            a, b = j - e, j
        else:
            a = rng.randint(n)
            b = rng.randint(n - 1)
            if b == a:
                b = (b + 1) % n
        for p in (a, b):
            if p not in used:
                used.add(p)
                inds.append(p)
                if len(inds) == s:
                    break
        tries += 1
    if len(inds) < s:
        while len(inds) < s:
            p = int(rng.randint(n))
            if p not in used:
                used.add(p)
                inds.append(p)
    inds = np.array(inds[:s], dtype=int)
    vals = np.empty(s)
    vals[:s // 2] = 1.0
    vals[s // 2:] = -1.0
    rng.shuffle(vals)
    return list(zip(inds.tolist(), vals.tolist()))

def gradient_dir(n, h, c, topc, k, rng):
    k = max(1, min(int(k), n // 2))
    if len(topc) == 0 or n < 4:
        return random_multi_dir(n, k, rng)
    act = min(40, len(topc))
    idx = topc[:act]
    cv = c[idx]
    temp = max(1e-6, 0.02 * (float(cv.max()) - float(cv.min())) + 1e-6)
    w = np.exp((cv - cv.max()) / temp)
    w = w / float(w.sum())
    G = np.zeros(n)
    for mi, wi in zip(idx, w):
        mi = int(mi)
        wi = float(wi)
        d = mi - (n - 1)
        if d == 0:
            G += wi * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        elif d > 0:
            G[:n - d] += wi * (1.0 - h[d:])
            G[d:] += -wi * h[:-d]
        else:
            e = -d
            G[e:] += wi * (1.0 - h[:-e])
            G[:n - e] += -wi * h[e:]
    raw = -G
    raw = raw - float(raw.mean())
    if not np.any(np.abs(raw) > 1e-14):
        return random_multi_dir(n, k, rng)
    order = np.argsort(raw)
    pos = order[-k:][::-1]
    neg = order[:k]
    plus = []
    minus = []
    used = set()
    for i in pos:
        i = int(i)
        if i not in used:
            used.add(i)
            plus.append(i)
            if len(plus) == k:
                break
    for i in neg:
        i = int(i)
        if i not in used:
            used.add(i)
            minus.append(i)
            if len(minus) == k:
                break
    while len(plus) < k or len(minus) < k:
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        if i in used:
            continue
        used.add(i)
        if len(plus) <= len(minus):
            plus.append(i)
        else:
            minus.append(i)
    return [(i, 1.0) for i in plus[:k]] + [(i, -1.0) for i in minus[:k]]

def stochastic_lines(h, c, n, deadline, rng, t0, budget_s):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    cur_h = h.copy()
    cur_c = np.correlate(cur_h, 1.0 - cur_h, mode="full")
    curC = float(cur_c.max()) * dx
    best_h = cur_h.copy()
    bestC = curC
    tsz = min(70, 2 * n - 1)
    topc = top_indices(cur_c, tsz)
    no_imp = 0
    accept = 0
    att = 0
    nkm = max(1, min(8, n // 10))
    while time.time() < deadline:
        frac = min(1.0, (time.time() - t0) / max(1.0, float(budget_s)))
        if frac < 0.30:
            st = 1.0
        elif frac < 0.65:
            st = 0.5
        elif frac < 0.90:
            st = 0.15
        else:
            st = 0.04
        r1 = rng.random_sample()
        r2 = rng.random_sample()
        if r2 < 0.50:
            k = 1
        elif r2 < 0.80:
            k = 2
        elif r2 < 0.95:
            k = 4
        else:
            k = nkm
        k = max(1, min(k, n // 2 if n > 2 else 1))
        if r1 < 0.30:
            v = gradient_dir(n, cur_h, cur_c, topc, max(2, k), rng)
        elif r1 < 0.70:
            v = biased_multi_dir(n, cur_c, topc, k, rng)
        else:
            v = random_multi_dir(n, k, rng)
        delta, new_c, new_cmax = line_search(cur_h, cur_c, v, n, st)
        newC = new_cmax * dx
        if abs(delta) > 1e-12 and newC < curC - 1e-12:
            for idx, val in v:
                nv = cur_h[idx] + delta * val
                if nv < 0.0:
                    nv = 0.0
                elif nv > 1.0:
                    nv = 1.0
                cur_h[idx] = nv
            cur_c = new_c
            curC = newC
            accept += 1
            if newC < bestC - 1e-12:
                bestC = newC
                best_h = cur_h.copy()
            no_imp = 0
            if accept % 80 == 0:
                cur_c = np.correlate(cur_h, 1.0 - cur_h, mode="full")
                curC = float(cur_c.max()) * dx
                if curC < bestC - 1e-12:
                    bestC = curC
                    best_h = cur_h.copy()
        else:
            no_imp += 1
        att += 1
        if att % 70 == 0:
            topc = top_indices(cur_c, tsz)
        if no_imp > 350 and time.time() > deadline - 60:
            base = best_h if rng.random_sample() < 0.7 else cur_h
            scale = 0.02 + 0.10 * rng.random_sample()
            cur_h = project_feasible(base + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n), n)
            cur_c = np.correlate(cur_h, 1.0 - cur_h, mode="full")
            curC = float(cur_c.max()) * dx
            topc = top_indices(cur_c, tsz)
            no_imp = 0
            if curC < bestC - 1e-12:
                bestC = curC
                best_h = cur_h.copy()
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    bc = float(np.max(np.correlate(best_h, 1.0 - best_h, mode="full"))) * dx
    ch = project_feasible(cur_h, n)
    cc = float(np.max(np.correlate(ch, 1.0 - ch, mode="full"))) * dx
    if cc < bc:
        best_h = ch
        bc = cc
    return best_h, float(bc)

def resample_h(h, n2):
    n1 = len(h)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n1, endpoint=False)
    x2 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n2, endpoint=False)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x2, x1, h), n2)

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    end = t0 + float(budget_s) - 4.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = len(h0)
    except Exception:
        n = 500
        h0 = np.full(n, 0.5)
    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n)
    c0 = np.correlate(h0, 1.0 - h0, mode="full")
    C0 = float(c0.max()) * (2.0 / n)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_c0 = c0
    bestC = C0
    alt_h = None
    alt_c = None
    altC = 1e9

    def eval_h(x):
        c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
        return c, float(c.max()) * (2.0 / len(x))

    sym = 0.5 * (h0 + (1.0 - h0[::-1]))
    sym = project_feasible(sym, n)
    cs, Cs = eval_h(sym)
    if Cs < bestC:
        alt_h, alt_c, altC = best_h, best_c0, bestC
        best_h, best_c0, bestC = sym, cs, Cs
    elif Cs < altC:
        alt_h, alt_c, altC = sym, cs, Cs

    thr = np.where(h0 > 0.5, 1.0, 0.0)
    thr = project_feasible(thr, n)
    ct, Ct = eval_h(thr)
    if Ct < bestC:
        alt_h, alt_c, altC = best_h, best_c0, bestC
        best_h, best_c0, bestC = thr, ct, Ct
    elif Ct < altC:
        alt_h, alt_c, altC = thr, ct, Ct

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    s1 = 0.5 + 0.45 * np.sin(np.pi * x)
    s1 = project_feasible(s1, n)
    c1, C1 = eval_h(s1)
    if C1 < bestC:
        alt_h, alt_c, altC = best_h, best_c0, bestC
        best_h, best_c0, bestC = s1, c1, C1
    elif C1 < altC:
        alt_h, alt_c, altC = s1, c1, C1

    main_deadline = t0 + max(90.0, 0.60 * float(budget_s) - 30.0)
    if main_deadline > end - 80.0:
        main_deadline = end - 80.0
    h2, C2 = stochastic_lines(best_h, best_c0, n, main_deadline, rng, t0, budget_s)
    if C2 < bestC:
        best_h, best_c0, bestC = h2, np.empty(0), C2

    if time.time() < end - 170.0 and alt_h is not None:
        d2 = min(end - 110.0, time.time() + 150.0)
        c_alt = np.correlate(alt_h, 1.0 - alt_h, mode="full")
        h3, C3 = stochastic_lines(alt_h, c_alt, n, d2, rng, t0, budget_s)
        if C3 < bestC:
            best_h, best_c0, bestC = h3, np.empty(0), C3

    if time.time() < end - 130.0:
        nb = len(best_h)
        n2 = 500 if nb != 500 else 900
        if 200 <= n2 <= 1200:
            hr = resample_h(best_h, n2)
            cr = np.correlate(hr, 1.0 - hr, mode="full")
            Cr = float(cr.max()) * (2.0 / n2)
            d3 = min(end - 70.0, time.time() + 100.0)
            h4, C4 = stochastic_lines(hr, cr, n2, d3, rng, t0, budget_s)
            if C4 < bestC:
                best_h, best_c0, bestC = h4, np.empty(0), C4

    if time.time() < end - 35.0:
        nf = len(best_h)
        cf = np.correlate(best_h, 1.0 - best_h, mode="full")
        h5, C5 = stochastic_lines(best_h, cf, nf, end - 6.0, rng, t0, budget_s)
        if C5 < bestC:
            best_h, bestC = h5, C5

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, len(best_h))
    nf = len(best_h)
    cf = np.correlate(best_h, 1.0 - best_h, mode="full")
    bestC = float(cf.max()) * (2.0 / nf)
    return best_h, float(bestC), int(nf)
```
